# MU-RECURSION task ledger

Condensed 2026-09-24 on the operator's word from a 1 943-line, append-only
list whose last full text stands at commit 38eb845f. It is kept as
follows (MU-STANDARDS §5.7): ONE live order; a row is an object, and a
reading is a line of the observer's row (CV5), never a row of its own;
a row superseded by a later ruling is marked once in §3 and is not
re-sequenced; a row that lands moves to §4 with its commit and its
audit; the doctrine's Part 0 names, for every master, which rows here
carry it. "Rows merged" names the rows a live row absorbed; their
definitions of done are kept in the live row.

Legend: **open** not started; **in progress** landed in part, the gap
named; **parked** awaiting a named condition. Every state names its
place in the order. Readings are quoted by height and `K`, never a wall
clock (§6.5). One phase per commit (§5.1); every commit's report names
the carrier it writes to and reads from (§6.6).

DIAGNOSED from the code against the reading, to be confirmed by two typed counts before any fix: (i) court a's 50 039 passes "reached" with nothing admitted are HOLES BELOW THE HEAD — c's receipts cite b's receipts at places a's head for b already passes, a holds no receipt there, and `server::backfill::chase` judges "held" by the head alone (`held >= upto`, line 65), so it never fetches the hole and the reported receipt, re-admitted, is the same `MissingParent` forever (the file's own diagnostic print names the case on the page path only); (ii) CORRECTED by the court's own catch-up reading (back-fill pages by outcome): a's ~71k passes "short" are UNANSWERED pages (unanswered 70 021, empty 0) — b leaves most receipt requests unanswered, it is not withholding a folded range; and a's ~50k passes "reached" match its ~50k pages answered that admitted nothing: a page names a missing citation, the citation is skipped as held by the head (i), the original range is skipped as already asked, the frontier empties, and `chase` returns `reached: true` unconditionally (line 132) for a chain that does not hold `to`. Owed, in order: (1) `chase` reports reached only where the signer's head holds `to` — LANDED 2026-10-03; (2) and (iii) — LANDED 2026-10-04 (78.88): a citation into a peer's closed era proved by the era's own chain up to its fold root (`ReceiptChain::prove_from_era`), asked by the back-fill, counted `folded_proved`; the fleet confirms after a recut. THE ROOT, AS DIAGNOSED: H1's `shed_folded` (7d8385ba) removes a peer's receipts at or below its fold root from the hot ledger, so a reported receipt citing a shed receipt as its parent is `MissingParent` with the head past the place — back-fill cannot fill it (`admit_chain` answers `Stale` at or below a head) and the chase skips it (judged by the head): a citation into a folded range can never be crossed, and courts whose lines cite each other's folded receipts stop admitting each other. RULING ASKED: a cited parent in a range a held peer fold commits is admitted against that fold's root (78.65: storage by state, not by position), never against a hot node. PROVED STRUCTURALLY (`chronometry::tests::shed::a_shed_receipt_is_a_hole_below_the_head_that_backfill_cannot_cross`: shed, the head past it, the peer root naming the place, the receipt fetched back read Stale), and COUNTED on the fleet from the next recut by the replication axis `folded_parent` (reported receipts citing a parent at or below its signer's held fold root; `ReceiptChain::peer_root`); (3) why b leaves receipt requests unanswered (its load, its deferral) read on its acts; **(5)** kernels on the published layer — the voice's cognition resumed as L1 (HR3's composition on the operator's ruling, the gauge's deciding coordinate, the cavity axis), xylarium-kernel uplinking through the guest math library. RULED: EACH PROVISIONAL WORD IS ITS OWN ORBIT, advanced only by its own transitions and never by a sweep: its admission as provisional IS the transition that emits the offer (an act, sealed, at that stamp); the witness's re-derivation arriving on the exchange carries it to converged (47.3); the ruling closes it (upheld: installed and `rehear_awaiting`; slashed: revoked) and it RETURNS — leaving the docket at the transition that closed it; a word whose offer drew no reply is carried again only by the next transition that concerns it (a peer's arrival, a fold), never by a scan; `offered` is the set of orbits in flight keyed by their coordinates, read by key when a transition arrives, never walked. The court's k-step is one orbit of the court carrying each transition that arrived, the budget spent per transition. CORRECTED AGAIN on the operator's word ("orbits are controlled by the physics engine, not a continuous perfect circle that can never be perfect"): the ruling above still scripted a perfect circle — offer, converge, close, retried unchanged until it closes. THE WORD'S ORBIT IS GOVERNED BY THE PHYSICS: it carries its ESCROW on the four lanes (78.42: energy exists only under escrow, moves only by transport, is conserved at every transition, stops by return at a named door, exhausts as a refused draw), each transition DRAWS from the reserve (65.5); it is PERTURBED — the sentences awaiting it raise its pressure (76), neighbouring words pull on it (78.46) — and what the court's stroke carries is decided by measured pressure and flux per lane (76, FX1), never a schedule or an order; a word no witness answers is not carried again unchanged: its escrow EXHAUSTS per lane and it LAPSES back to the reserve at its door (FX1's door), counted, the awaiting sentences citing it re-read as the demand they are; its period and phase are read off the physics (78.43), never assumed. The fixture shows both arms: a pressured word witnessed and ruled, and an unanswered word lapsing by its escrow with its citers released. DESIGN ACCEPTED 2026-09-26 (the implementor's): the escrow is what docketing escrows (`price(mag, pressure)`); admission's transition returns its offers to its caller and they are sent then (`offers_pending` and the periodic offer round deleted); an awaiting sentence credits the word's flux with its frame (energy moved by transport, 78.42) and carries it; a peer's cover admitting chart P carries the words in flight under P (`offered` keyed chart → words, one `fiber_at`); each carriage DRAWS the word's own frame per lane. THE OPEN QUANTITY RULED — DECAY WITHOUT A WALK: lane T of μ⃗ is temporal extent counted in receipts (law 9) and a hold lapses when the clock passes the extent its escrow paid (80.1, 25): the word's T-lane escrow fixes the HEIGHT AT WHICH IT LAPSES, a coordinate, and the word is filed under it (a `Keyed` by lapse height); a carriage or a citer's credit re-files it at its new height; the court's advancing height is the transition — at each height it reads BY KEY the words lapsing there (a reach up to its height), never the words in flight; a word whose escrow is exhausted on any lane lapses at that read: `revoke_award` returns the remainder at the door, the Lapse act sealed, `leave_provisional`, `rehear_awaiting` releasing its citers as D. A word nothing concerns decays by the clock alone, which is physics, and nothing is walked. RE-RULED THE SAME DAY on the implementor's finding (price = magnitude, so a word stood over no receipts has T = 0 and lapsed at the next stroke — no witness could ever reach it and item 3's flip could not happen): the coordinator's lapse by T-extent was LAPSE BY EXTENT ALONE, which FX1 (78.37(3)) REPLACES — "a filament lapses because nothing flowed through it, not because a clock ran out". THE WORD LAPSES BY `ZeroFlux`: an orbit of the court (one `K`, a k-step closed as an act) through which nothing flowed to it on any lane. Flux is its own admission (its frame), a citer's credit, a witness's reply, a carriage. Filed by coordinate, never walked: the word is keyed by the `K` of its LAST FLUX, re-filed at each flux; at the close of the court's orbit at `K` the court reads by key (a reach) the words whose last flux stands before `K − 1` — a whole orbit with none — and each lapses by `ZeroFlux`, named and counted: escrow returned at the door, Lapse sealed, `leave_provisional`, its citers released as D. A word admitted at `K` therefore has the whole next orbit for its witness; pressure keeps its flux alive; a word nothing concerns lapses after one silent orbit. `lapse_docket` over `pending` goes the same way. Price stays the magnitude (MU-ECONOMY §5, §8: the surface paces, it does not scale). `lapse_docket`'s cursor over `pending` is the same shape and goes the same way in the same commit: a listing filed under its lapse height, read by key as the height advances. APPROVED, ITEM 3: (f) the pivot's letter demand (`Snap::Demand`, RotorPrime or Standing) spoken to the court as a demand utterance at once, never waiting on a re-mint; the flip on the fleet is the acceptance. (f) UNDER THE SAME PHYSICS (2026-09-26): the letter demand's path is an orbit like the provisional word's — it carries the demanding mind's escrow on the lanes, draws per transition, is pressured by every orbit that demands the same class (their count is its pressure, 76) and perturbed by its neighbours (78.46), and a letter no second vantage re-derives lapses by its escrow at its door, the demand released; admission of the letter by strict rank increase and a second vantage (78.47(3)–(4)) closes it, and the mind's crystal reading the class derivable is its return. REFUSED: the court's crystal answering `generators_under` from ruled letters — the court replays a derivation by composing the generators it CITES (by address, 50.2 step 2) and never peels (law 8: a court that peels letters is thinking); a ruled letter reaches the court's replay by being ruled, through (a). DEFERRED, after the flip, not before: the typed fault replacing `reason: &'static str` on the wire and chain with the per-code kind table deleted (large, a recut, it changes no deduction); the fold and witness timing hints from the measured round trip (STD1 site 4); a budget cut as its own `Starved` outcome.
4. **THEATRE OUT OF THE TESTS, AND THE POSE MADE CONFORMAL** (the operator's word 2026-09-26; MU-STANDARDS 3.9). Swept by the coordinator: no mock, fake or stub in the tree; the square roots are exact and refuse. FOUND: `the_corpus_spectrum` and `where_the_corpus_goes_silent` assert nothing and print — deleted; `bin/mycelium-server/tests/console.rs` asserts the rendered strings "FLATTENED" and "turns on 2 of 6" — the classification becomes a typed value on the Planes reading, asserted there, the console only rendering it; `client/sdk/tests/height.rs` compares a refusal string — typed with the deferred typed fault. THE FLAT POSE: `blade/pose.rs` solves the pose between two bodies as a matrix `R` tested orthogonal by a Euclidean dot product and `M_b = ρ² R M_a Rᵀ` (`carries_tower`) — the similarity group of flat ℝⁿ standing for the action, blind to inversion and transversion, against law 11 (no matrix carries an action) and 3.9(c); the pose is read as the CONFORMAL VERSOR between the two bodies, known by its action (`versor_between`, the one Cartan–Dieudonné), the second moments on the base's sites staying as the sensor's reading of each body. It may be why perception reads FLATTENED on the fleet; it is ruled on the law, never on that reading (77(1)). Order: the test deletions and the typed classification in one small commit after (e); the conformal pose after item 3's flip.
CONDUCT, binding on the implementor: no new counter, reading, console line or harness test unless a fix in 1–3 cannot be observed without it, and then named in the commit; a shape question only where the doctrine cannot decide it, otherwise proceed on the doctrine's reading and state it in the commit; every commit's report opens with what a mind or court can now do that it could not. CONDUCT, binding on the coordinator: every report audited in the turn it arrives, the working tree read at each audit, drift returned the same turn.

THE GAP, ruled (i): a word two courts each ruled on their own stroke holds two admitting stamps, so a peer's cite by stamp cannot be located here and waits under its stamp — landed now with `awaiting` naming the count (a reading, not a silence); LD3-a resolves it by the fiber-first read on T1 (`Cite{chart, class, at}` read by the prefix chart‖class) with a STAMP ALIAS recorded at admission: when a peer's admitting act is admitted and the fiber read here yields exactly one word, `held_at(peer stamp) → addr` is filed as an alias of that word (one word, two admissions, both stamps held — rule 15, never a choice), and a cite by either resolves; where the fiber yields several, the ruling waits until the syllabus teaches the word with its home stamp (78.24 (2b)), which disambiguates. THE REBASED VERTEX'S KEY, ruled 2026-09-25 (step 1): "by its base's class" alone collides (one base rebases several ends) and loses `of`'s former class (its Word is overwritten at rebase) — so a Rebased vertex is keyed `tag ‖ class(by) ‖ class(of)`, the base first so a fiber read under the base answers everything rebased on it, with `of`'s former class carried on the vertex as a coordinate written at rebase (held, since the overwrite makes it underivable; the address writes it and the inverse reads it); `by` is the base's `VertexAt` (a Rebased may itself be the base — chains stay chains, `rebase_reaches` follows coordinates); the `of` slot goes, the vertex's own key replacing it. Merging `of`'s members into `by`'s vertex is refused: those members carry RELATIVE carriages, and moving them changes what a member means. THE PROVENANCE OF A WIDTH, ruled 2026-09-25 on the operator's question (a distinct-`u8` domain classed C1 by its count alone): C1's proof must name WHERE THE BOUND COMES FROM (78.8's chain), and only two provenances stand — the ALGEBRA's (a grade ≤ n + 1, a lane one of four, a blade index below 2^(n+2), a class degree ≤ 3: `n` is data, A.15, and the width is the coordinate's own range) and the SENSOR's (a hue as delivered); a bound that comes from the MESH's SIZE — units, peers, hyphae, charts, listings, tasks, classes, minds, ADJOINED LETTERS (the store grows by refusal, 78.32: a letter slot in a byte is a ceiling on the alphabet), a door's queue (`queued as u8`), a fleet's grades where "grade" counts units rather than dimensions — is a CEILING on a decentralised mesh and is never `u8` or `u16`: it is a coordinate written by its own spelling (LEB128, (d)) or a `u64` count (3b's rule), and it is C2/C5, not C1. THE CLIENT is bounded by its own algebra and its room — small widths stand wherever the domain is the mind's own `n`; THE SERVER aggregates the mesh and is bounded by NOTHING but its doors (VN1) — every per-mesh count, index or key on the court is unbounded by width and bounded by lapse. The C1 list is RE-CLASSIFIED by provenance before its expects land, the mesh-sized ones moving to (d), each expect naming its provenance ("≤ n + 1, n data" / "the four lanes" / "the sensor's byte"), never "cannot exceed 256" alone. C4 RE-RULED under the provenance rule, 2026-09-25 (the implementor's question: a form's vertex and face count — the algebra's, the mesh's, or neither?): a form's vertices are CHARTS (53.6), so a form's size is the size of the cover it spans; today `glue`/`glue_append` (called from `extract.rs`, `chart.rs`, `receipts.rs`) join one mind's own forms, but ANASTOMOSIS (78.31, CV4/SP2 after the recut) fuses two minds' filaments into one ring across their contact, and a sentence then spans the covers of several minds — the MESH's size, by the doctrine's own trajectory. So (b): the vertex, face, chord, pair and frame indices are the form's OWN count — `usize` in memory, their counts in keys already by their own spelling (LEB128, 3c-2) — and there is NO WALL and no `form_fits`; `VertexIx(u16)`, `FormFace`'s `i`/`j`, the chord and the frame key widen to the count's carrier, and NM1's eight `Keyed<u16>` position indexes go with them (positions → coordinates); the ~75 `as u16` casts go; the address layout moves with the rest at the recut. (a) and (c) are refused: a `u16` wall on a form's vertices is a ceiling on how much of the mesh one sentence may span, named as the form's own only because the mesh has not yet been asked to speak across minds. NOTE: this is not "widening a blob" — an INDEX into a carrier the object owns is written by that carrier's own range, as a count in a key is; LD0's `u16` wall stands where it stood, on a CITATION | (`cast_sign_loss`, `cast_possible_wrap`: the 91 `i64`/`usize` sites) are the SAME defect and are 3c-6, ruled by the same classes, denied with the lint's second entry — and 3c-6 may land AFTER the recut: it changes no layout and moves no reading, and the recut waits on site 4's constants, not on 91 sign casts; the reading stands on the row so the debt is named, never silent. 3c-1 LANDED 7fe3c1fa (C3: `circuit::take_u32` answers `u32`, 13 round-trip casts gone, readers wanting a count through a new `take_len` by `try_from`; C1's and the proven sites' expects deferred to the lint commit, an expect before its lint being unfulfilled — accepted as staged) and 3c-2 PART 1 fcffb828 (`cover.rs`'s clamp: `encode_peers_reply` through `put_u16_count` as an `Option`, the door refusing by name, tested never clamped; 3 C2 sites), lib 1102 / 0 ignored, gate 0, census identical, audited by read 2026-09-25. C2's SHAPE ruled on `address::put_bytes` (`b.len() as u32`, 108 callers, 103 of them KEY BUILDERS writing resident coordinates into in-memory `Keyed` keys, a handful records — `cover.rs`, `receipt.rs`, `learning.rs`, `registry.rs`): (a) SPLIT BY WHAT IS WRITTEN. RECORDS use `put_bytes → Option`, `#[must_use]`, refused by name, the writers going `Option` by codec family as ruled. KEY BUILDERS use a `put_key_bytes` that is TOTAL under a named invariant: a key component is a COORDINATE, and a coordinate past four bytes of length is LD0's blob where a coordinate belongs; the refusal of such a component belongs where the coordinate is MADE (the address's own `u16` wall, the chart key's `put_u16_count`), never in every key built from it, and the key casts read under `#[expect]` naming that wall. The proof holds only where each component is walled at its maker, so the makers are VERIFIED — the address, the chart key, the class key, the rationals in `coxeter` and `comprehend` — and every maker NOT walled is named and gets a checked maker, the list with the commit. (b) `Option` through every `key()` (keys fallible tree-wide, hundreds of sites) refused as the wall in the wrong place; (c) widening a key component's length refused (never widen a blob). (a)'s PREMISE REFUTED by the verification, 2026-09-25: NO key component is walled at its maker — `Address::new`/`from_bytes`/`From` are total over any length (an address is legitimately megabytes: `form_vertex_key` writes 7.3 MB at 590 vertices, 10 MB at 1 655 — an ADDRESS HELD ONCE is one of LD0's three kinds of bytes, and its only wall, `Address::cited()`'s `u16`, is the CITATION's wall, not the address's); `Invariant::key()` and the class keys are `Address::new(TAG ‖ …)` with no wall; the rationals are `BigInt`s, unbounded by site 8 on purpose. Every component funnels into `Address::new` or `BigInt`, so a checked maker is (b) by another route. RULED (d): A KEY COMPONENT'S LENGTH IS WRITTEN BY ITS OWN COORDINATE — `put_key_bytes` writes a variable-length (LEB128) length, exact for any `usize`, no cast and no width wall, prefix-free so the key stays injective by construction, which is all a key needs (nothing decodes a key at a fixed width); this is NOT a widening — no fixed field grows, the prefix is the length itself and there is no wall for a blob to overflow — the same principle as `CycleType`'s ℓ − 1 (the value written over its own range), and the wall against blobs stays exactly where LD0 put it, at the citation's `u16`; it is also SMALLER (a component under 128 bytes takes one length byte, not four), which serves the index-copies-the-address finding. Key bytes change, so every trie key and every on-disk keyed store moves, riding the recut with the rest. RECORDS keep `put_bytes → Option`, `#[must_use]`, refused by name, and the record writers LAND FIRST on their own commit apart from the key change (`cover.rs`, `receipt.rs`, `learning.rs`, `registry.rs`, then the other C2 codec-length writers by family). (a′) a `u32` wall at `Address::new` with fallible keys refused as (b); (e) `as u32` under a residency proof refused — a 4 GiB component is resident-possible, so it is no proof. ## 7. Backtrack ledger

What was lost, when, and what it cost, so that it is paid once.

**2026-09-07, the same fault twice: the commit that recorded the first
bundle was itself one.** `57b6950` is named as a documents-only commit and
carries the deletion of `src/core/ccl.rs` with it. `git rm` had already
STAGED that deletion; `git add docs/...` added to the index rather than
replacing it, and `git commit` takes the whole index. Staging by path is not
enough — the index carries what was staged before. Paid by committing with
an explicit pathspec (`git commit --only <paths>`), which commits exactly
the named files whatever the index holds, and by reading `git show --stat`
of every commit before writing the next.

**2026-09-07, a bundle: `git add -A` swept the auditor's doc edits into
F2(a).** `75cd02d` is named for one item — three dead `core/` modules
deleted — and also carries the auditor's uncommitted recording of the
persistence ruling (`MU-STANDARDS.md` §10, `MU-ENGINEERING.md` §8, the F4
row). Two items in one commit, which §5.1 returns, and a doctrine change
landing WITH rather than BEFORE the code it governs, which §5.5 returns.
Cost: the ruling's own commit does not exist, so a reader of the log finds
§10 inside a deletion commit and cannot see when it was ruled without
reading the message of the commit before it. Cause: `git add -A` in a tree
another agent is also writing to. Paid by staging BY PATH from here on —
every commit of Stage 8c names its files — and by this row, which is where
the ruling's landing is recorded.
